## Controlled boundary scenarios

| Week | Trigger | Action | Reward | Drives |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | thoughtful two-recipient message younger than 24h | independent observer chimes in once | cost 2 allowance; 20 giver XP; 8 qualifying coins; two recipient stickers; no Crowd booster | CD1/5; W/M; R; CD6 allowance |
| 1 | giver/recipient, duplicate, age 25h, or insufficient budget | attempt chime-in or remove and re-add | no additional award/reward; removal changes nothing | CD2/5; W/M; L/R |
| 1 | 3-person ring; notes padded to five words | third member endorses the other two; rotate roles, within all budgets | 225 base XP per member/week at 15 given and 15 received; chime-in quests can be farmed without self-chime | CD2/4/5; M + W progress; L/R; sincerity risk |
| 2 | one member leaves an incomplete group quest | retain original eligible roster/target for this period | no completion caused by denominator shrink; next period may use current roster; proposal | CD1/2/5; W/M; L/R |
| 2 | unthanked member leaves two-page album | G34 reevaluates current roster | album completes without new thanks; retain historical completion but do not describe it as new thoughtful work | CD2/4/5; W/M; L/R |
| 2 | completed week-1 quest; claim arrives in week 2 and retries | claim stable entitlement ID once; replay reveal | 15 coins once; week-1 reward remains claimable; animation has no ledger effect; proposal | CD2/3/4; W/M; L/R; removes CD8 expiry |
